<a class="thumb" href="https://www.economist.com/interactive/briefing/2026/09/03/nvidia-is-the-central-bank-of-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=economist.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>economist.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">157</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.economist.com/interactive/briefing/2026/09/03/nvidia-is-the-central-bank-of-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia is the central bank of AI</span><span class='zh'>英伟达是AI的央行</span></a><span class="dom">economist.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s dominance in AI compute gives it outsized influence over model training, pricing, and access.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达在AI计算力上的垄断地位，让其对模型训练、定价与准入拥有举足轻重的影响力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nvidia’s dominance in AI compute gives it outsized influence over model training, pricing, and access. As demand for accelerators grows, the company effectively sets the monetary policy of AI, shaping which startups succeed and which research directions are viable. This centrality invites regulatory and competitive scrutiny with broad ripple effects across ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达在AI计算力上的垄断地位，让其对模型训练、定价与准入拥有举足轻重的影响力。随着对加速器的需求攀升，这家公司实质上在制定AI的货币政策，左右哪些初创企业能生存、哪些研究方向可行。其高度集中性也引发对市场竞争和供应链的关注与潜在风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google blocks Aurora Store, limiting access for GrapheneOS users who rely on Aurora as an alternative to Google Play Services.</span><span class='zh'>Google 封禁 Aurora Store，影响依赖 Aurora 作为 Google 服务替代的 GrapheneOS 用户。</span></p>

This move heightens tensions between privacy-focused Android forks and Google&#x27;s control of app distribution. It underscores ongoing battles over who gets to decide what runs on Android devices.</span><span class='zh'>Google 封禁 Aurora Store，影响依赖 Aurora 作为 Google 服务替代的 GrapheneOS 用户。此举加剧了隐私导向的 Android 派系与 Google 对应用分发控制之间的博弈。也凸显了对 Android 生态由谁来决定可运行软件的持续争议。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.mozilla.org/en/firefox/ad-blocker-on-ios/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Introducing Ad Blocker for Firefox on iOS</span><span class='zh'>在 iOS 的 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能</span></a><span class="dom">blog.mozilla.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Firefox on iOS gains an integrated ad blocker, expanding privacy controls on Apple&#x27;s platform.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 在 iOS 版 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能，增强对广告与跟踪的控制，改善隐私与浏览体验。</span></p>

It demonstrates ongoing improvements to iOS web tooling that bypasses some mobile ad economics.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 在 iOS 版 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能，增强对广告与跟踪的控制，改善隐私与浏览体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/09/draftkings-using-ai-supercharge-harms-online-behavioral-advertising" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to behaviorally target chronic gamblers</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在用 AI 针对慢性赌徒进行行为定向</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending.</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending. This raises urgent ethical and regulatory concerns about predatory targeting, calling for stronger safeguards, transparency, and oversight to protect vulnerable users.</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。这一做法引发关于掠夺性定向、伦理与监管的紧迫关切，需加强保护措施、透明度和监管以保护脆弱用户。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wired.com/story/hackers-flock-camera-data-shows-how-system-works/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6aa9a89861c11f99a88f271a/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Hackers%20Got%20Inside%20a%20Flock%20Camera.%20Its%20Data%20Shows%20How%20the%20System%20Really%20Works.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wired.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">161</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/hackers-flock-camera-data-shows-how-system-works/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers Got Inside a Flock Camera</span><span class='zh'>黑客入侵 Flock 摄像头，其数据揭示系统运作方式</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hackers breached a Flock camera, and the retrieved data reveals how the system authenticates, communicates with the cloud, and handles footage.</span><span class='zh'>黑客入侵了 Flock 摄像头，窃取的数据揭示了该系统如何进行认证、与云端通信以及如何处理影像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hackers breached a Flock camera, and the retrieved data reveals how the system authenticates, communicates with the cloud, and handles footage. The leak provides a rare inside look at the telemetry and control signals powering modern surveillance hardware, highlighting potential weaknesses in device-to-cloud architectures. It underscores the need for stronger firmware integrity and better security practices in IoT surveillance.</span><span class='zh'>黑客入侵了 Flock 摄像头，窃取的数据揭示了该系统如何进行认证、与云端通信以及如何处理影像。此举让人得以窥见现代监控硬件背后的遥测与控制信号，揭示设备与云端架构中的潜在弱点。它强调需要加强固件完整性和物联网监控的安全实践。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://en.sedaily.com/finance/2026/09/20/samsung-to-double-hbm4-output-next-year-sources-say" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Samsung is expected to more than double output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM</span><span class='zh'>三星HBM4/HBM4E DRAM产量有望翻倍以上</span></a><span class="dom">en.sedaily.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Samsung is reportedly set to more than double the output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM next year to meet surging AI and data-center demand.</span><span class='zh'>三星计划在来年把 HBM4 与 HBM4E DRAM 的产量大幅提升，超过现有水平的两倍，以满足 AI 与数据中心对高带宽内存的需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Samsung is reportedly set to more than double the output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM next year to meet surging AI and data-center demand. The planned capacity ramp could help ease tight supply for high-bandwidth memory used in GPUs and accelerators, potentially impacting pricing and supply chains across hyperscalers.</span><span class='zh'>三星计划在来年把 HBM4 与 HBM4E DRAM 的产量大幅提升，超过现有水平的两倍，以满足 AI 与数据中心对高带宽内存的需求。这一扩产将缓解相关供需紧张，可能影响 GPU/加速器市场的定价与供应。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://www.techdirt.com/2026/09/03/hackers-had-a-live-feed-of-every-id-this-verification-company-scanned-for-over-a-year/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers had a live feed of every ID verification company scanned for over a year</span><span class='zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流持续一年多</span></a><span class="dom">techdirt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers reveal that attackers maintained a live stream of every ID verification company&#x27;s scanned data for more than a year, exposing systemic privacy and security risks in identity verification ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流保持了超过一年的持续访问，暴露身份验证生态中的隐私和安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers reveal that attackers maintained a live stream of every ID verification company&#x27;s scanned data for more than a year, exposing systemic privacy and security risks in identity verification ecosystems. The breach underscores how third-party verification pipelines can leak sensitive data and why rigorous monitoring and supply-chain controls matter.</span><span class='zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流保持了超过一年的持续访问，暴露身份验证生态中的隐私和安全风险。事件凸显了第三方验证链路的脆弱性，以及加强监控与供应链控制的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pirateface.co/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pirate Face Rescues LLM Models from Deletion</span><span class='zh'>Pirate Face 拯救被删除的语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">pirateface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pirate Face promotes archiving and rehosting LLMs that are at risk of removal, arguing for preservation of research artifacts and open access.</span><span class='zh'>Pirate Face 旨在存档并重新托管可能被删除的语言模型，主张保存研究成果并促进开放获取。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pirate Face promotes archiving and rehosting LLMs that are at risk of removal, arguing for preservation of research artifacts and open access. The project highlights a broader tension between platform takedowns and community-maintained backups, with implications for licensing, provenance, and long-tail AI research.</span><span class='zh'>Pirate Face 旨在存档并重新托管可能被删除的语言模型，主张保存研究成果并促进开放获取。该项目凸显了平台下架与社区维护备份之间的张力，对许可、来源和长期 AI 研究具有深远影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.alexewerlof.com/p/coding-is-not-solved" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Coding is not solved</span><span class='zh'>编码问题尚未解决</span></a><span class="dom">blog.alexewerlof.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging.</span><span class='zh'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging. AI can help but cannot replace the craft of software engineering or the need for disciplined practices.</span><span class='zh'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。AI 能帮助，但无法取代软件工程的工艺性与规范性实践。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://definitelynotwindows.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Windows 11½</span><span class='zh'>Windows 11½（幽默笔记）</span></a><span class="dom">definitelynotwindows.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.aymannadeem.com/artificial/intelligence,/developer/tools/2026/09/24/plan-mode-is-dead.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Plan mode is dead</span><span class='zh'>计划模式已死</span></a><span class="dom">aymannadeem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that explicit &#x27;plan mode&#x27; for AI agents is no longer necessary; modern agents can effectively operate via tool integration and dynamic prompting without a separate planning step.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 AI 代理中的显式“计划模式”已不再必要；通过工具集成和动态提示，代理可以在没有独立规划阶段的情况下工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that explicit &#x27;plan mode&#x27; for AI agents is no longer necessary; modern agents can effectively operate via tool integration and dynamic prompting without a separate planning step. The shift reduces overhead but requires robust tool ecosystems and guardrails.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 AI 代理中的显式“计划模式”已不再必要；通过工具集成和动态提示，代理可以在没有独立规划阶段的情况下工作。此转变降低了开销，但需要更健全的工具生态和监管机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://security.apple.com/blog/apple-reference-image/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Reference Image: A New Approach for Verified Photography</span><span class='zh'>苹果参考图像：用于认证摄影的新方法</span></a><span class="dom">security.apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple unveils the Reference Image approach to verify photography provenance on devices, enabling cryptographic-style checks and image-forensics workflows.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出参考图像，用以验证摄影的来源与真实性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple unveils the Reference Image approach to verify photography provenance on devices, enabling cryptographic-style checks and image-forensics workflows. The goal is to harden trust in photos, making spoofed or manipulated imagery easier to detect at capture or processing time. This strategy could reshape how apps and services verify media authenticity in practice.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出参考图像，用以验证摄影的来源与真实性。通过对比参考信息，系统可更早检测伪造或篡改的照片，提升数字影像的可信度。此方法可能改变媒体真实性的验证标准与流程。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Real luxuries aren&#x27;t gadgets or wealth.</span><span class='zh'>真正的奢侈不是金钱或物质，而是时间、健康和意义深厚的人际关系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Real luxuries aren&#x27;t gadgets or wealth. Time, health, and meaningful relationships are what truly matter, and the piece urges founders to protect these assets as they build.</span><span class='zh'>真正的奢侈不是金钱或物质，而是时间、健康和意义深厚的人际关系。文章劝创业者在追求成就的同时，优先保护这些最宝贵的资产。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A major rail disruption in the Netherlands appears to be caused by suspected sabotage, as authorities investigate the motive and method. The incident underscores how critical rail networks remain vulnerable to targeted disruption and how operators balance rapid service restoration with security in signaling and infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>荷兰出现大规模铁路中断，调查初步指向疑似蓄意破坏。此事件凸显铁路网络对有针对性的破坏仍然脆弱，并在修复与安全之间带来新的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cnn.com/2026/09/18/politics/us-military-ai-false-intelligence-china-ship" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Military had close call after using AI for hallucinated intelligence report</span><span class='zh'>美国军方因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些行动</span></a><span class="dom">cnn.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The U.S. military nearly acted on an AI-generated intelligence report that turned out to be hallucinated; this incident underscores the danger of trusting AI outputs in high-stakes operations and the importance of human vetting.</span><span class='zh'>美军因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些据此行动，凸显高风险场景下对AI输出进行人工核验的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The U.S. military nearly acted on an AI-generated intelligence report that turned out to be hallucinated; this incident underscores the danger of trusting AI outputs in high-stakes operations and the importance of human vetting. Officials are recalibrating procedures, with emphasis on stronger verification and safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>美军因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些据此行动，凸显高风险场景下对AI输出进行人工核验的重要性。相关方正加强验证流程、人机协同与安全措施来降低此类风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wsj.com/world/middle-east/aws-says-it-cant-restore-some-data-from-mideast-facilities-struck-by-iran-ddcb7e5d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AWS says it can&#x27;t restore some data from mideast facilities struck by Iran</span><span class='zh'>AWS：中东设施受袭后部分数据无法恢复</span></a><span class="dom">wsj.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AWS says some data cannot be restored after Iran-struck Middle East facilities, underscoring limits of disaster recovery and the value of cross-region backups.</span><span class='zh'>AWS 表示在被伊朗袭击的中东设施中，部分数据无法恢复，凸显灾备的局限性，以及跨区域备份的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mcpherrin.ca/2026/09/07/rsa.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;ve factored the RSA keys of a Certificate Authority from the 90s</span><span class='zh'>我已经分解了90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥</span></a><span class="dom">mcpherrin.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Factoring a 1990s-era CA RSA key underscores how legacy cryptography remains a risk.</span><span class='zh'>对90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥进行分解，揭示了旧式加密的长期风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Factoring a 1990s-era CA RSA key underscores how legacy cryptography remains a risk. The disclosure prompts re-issuance of certificates under stronger keys, revocation considerations, and a push toward modern algorithms and shorter key lifetimes.</span><span class='zh'>对90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥进行分解，揭示了旧式加密的长期风险。此事可能推动重新颁发证书、吊销受影响的证书，并促使向更强的算法和更短的密钥寿命过渡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The EPA is weighing changes that would ease or remove public comment requirements on data center pollution permits.</span><span class='zh'>环保署正在评估可能放宽或取消数据中心污染许可的公众审查规则。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EPA is weighing changes that would ease or remove public comment requirements on data center pollution permits. The development signals tension between rapid data-center growth and watchdog oversight.</span><span class='zh'>环保署正在评估可能放宽或取消数据中心污染许可的公众审查规则。批评者警告，透明度下降会将环境风险从社区转移，而支持者则认为简化许可有助于基础设施建设。这一动向凸显数据中心快速扩张与监督之间的博弈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wax_motor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wax motor</span><span class='zh'>蜡动机</span></a><span class="dom">en.wikipedia.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A wax motor uses the expansion of paraffin wax when heated to produce motion, offering quiet operation and low maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>蜡动机通过加热使石蜡膨胀来产生运动，工作安静、维护成本低，广泛用于恒温器和阀门控制等场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A wax motor uses the expansion of paraffin wax when heated to produce motion, offering quiet operation and low maintenance. It is commonly used in thermostats and valve control, providing a simple, durable actuation method with trade-offs in response time and temperature sensitivity.</span><span class='zh'>蜡动机通过加热使石蜡膨胀来产生运动，工作安静、维护成本低，广泛用于恒温器和阀门控制等场景。其简单耐用的驱动方式虽成本低，但受温度和响应时间限制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/InfinityLoop1308/PipePipe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PipePipe: NewPipe hard fork implementing SponsorBlock</span><span class='zh'>PipePipe：NewPipe 的硬分叉，实现 SponsorBlock</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PipePipe is a hard fork of NewPipe that integrates SponsorBlock directly, enabling automatic skipping of sponsor segments in videos.</span><span class='zh'>PipePipe 是对 NewPipe 的硬分叉，内置 SponsorBlock 功能，能够自动跳过视频中的赞助段落。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PipePipe is a hard fork of NewPipe that integrates SponsorBlock directly, enabling automatic skipping of sponsor segments in videos. This move concentrates user-side ad-skipping controls in an open-source mobile YouTube client, with potential effects on monetization and creator funding models. It also shows how forked open-source projects can quickly adopt niche features.</span><span class='zh'>PipePipe 是对 NewPipe 的硬分叉，内置 SponsorBlock 功能，能够自动跳过视频中的赞助段落。此举将广告跳过控制权带入开源移动端应用，可能影响创作者的收益与平台生态，同时体现开源社区对定制功能的需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A federal appeals court confirms government contractors must treat Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, reinforcing risk-management and vendor due-diligence requirements.</span><span class='zh'>美国上诉法院维持对Anthropic的供应链风险认定，要求在政府采购中加强对该供应商的风险评估。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A federal appeals court confirms government contractors must treat Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, reinforcing risk-management and vendor due-diligence requirements. For AI developers and buyers, that feeds into compliance burdens, eligibility for sensitive work, and potential shifts in who can bid on government AI projects.</span><span class='zh'>美国上诉法院维持对Anthropic的供应链风险认定，要求在政府采购中加强对该供应商的风险评估。对AI公司和政府买方意味着增加合规负担、影响参与敏感项目的资格，以及对政府合同的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jaredpalmer/kev/tree/main" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kev: Tiny Jev-like family of decision models built on top of Qwen3.5</span><span class='zh'>Kev：基于 Qwen3.5 构建的微型 Jev 风格决策模型家族</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kev is a tiny family of Jev-like decision models built on top of Qwen3.5.</span><span class='zh'>Kev 是一组基于 Qwen3.5 的微型 Jev 风格决策模型家族。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kev is a tiny family of Jev-like decision models built on top of Qwen3.5. The project aims to provide lightweight, modular decision-making capabilities that sit atop a capable LLM, enabling quick experimentation with planning and reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>Kev 是一组基于 Qwen3.5 的微型 Jev 风格决策模型家族。该项目旨在提供轻量化、模块化的决策能力，使开发者可以在强大的大模型上快速尝试规划与推理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/iczelia/bzip3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>bzip3</span><span class='zh'>bzip3：新一代无损压缩格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>bzip3 is a new lossless compression format designed to outperform existing algorithms like bz2 in speed and compression ratio.</span><span class='zh'>bzip3 是一种新型无损压缩格式，目标是在速度与压缩比方面优于 bz2 等现有算法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>bzip3 is a new lossless compression format designed to outperform existing algorithms like bz2 in speed and compression ratio. It aims to integrate with existing tooling and influence archival and software distribution.</span><span class='zh'>bzip3 是一种新型无损压缩格式，目标是在速度与压缩比方面优于 bz2 等现有算法。它设计为兼容现有工具链，有望影响归档和软件分发的效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>StreetComplete on iOS is now in public beta</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete iOS 公测现已开启</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks.</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks. The move broadens access beyond Android and could accelerate map data improvements, though testers should expect typical beta churn as the app matures.</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。此次扩平台可望推动社区贡献并让 Android 之外的用户加入，但测试阶段仍会有常见的不稳定和本地化需要改进的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/devdotfast/whiteboard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Whiteboard (YC W26) – An open-source IDE for thoughtful software design</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Whiteboard（YC W26）——用于深思软件设计的开源 IDE</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Whiteboard is an open-source IDE focused on thoughtful software design, prioritizing architecture reasoning and collaboration over mere code editing.</span><span class='zh'>Whiteboard 是一个开源 IDE，强调软件设计的深思熟虑与团队协作，而不仅仅是编写代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Whiteboard is an open-source IDE focused on thoughtful software design, prioritizing architecture reasoning and collaboration over mere code editing. If adopted widely, it could push design-first tooling and improve team alignment early in the development process.</span><span class='zh'>Whiteboard 是一个开源 IDE，强调软件设计的深思熟虑与团队协作，而不仅仅是编写代码。若被广泛采用，可能推动设计优先的工具生态并帮助团队在开发早期就对齐决策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/reladraw/reladraw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Reladraw – A diagram language where you decide where to place things</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Reladraw — 一个由你决定放置元素的图表语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reladraw introduces a diagram language where layout decisions are explicit, letting users specify where nodes and components go instead of relying on automatic layout heuristics.</span><span class='zh'>Reladraw 引入一种图表语言，布局位置由用户显式指定，不再完全依赖自动排版。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reladraw introduces a diagram language where layout decisions are explicit, letting users specify where nodes and components go instead of relying on automatic layout heuristics. This could enable more deterministic rendering, easier versioning of diagrams, and tooling that translates data into visuals for developers and designers building complex diagrams.</span><span class='zh'>Reladraw 引入一种图表语言，布局位置由用户显式指定，不再完全依赖自动排版。这有助于实现可重复的图形结果、便于版本控制，并为将数据直接转化为可视化的工具链提供新可能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/git-bug/git-bug" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Git-bug: Distributed, offline-first bug tracker embedded in Git</span><span class='zh'>Git-bug：嵌入式 Git 的分布式离线优先缺陷跟踪器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Git-bug is a distributed, offline-first bug tracker embedded in Git, letting issues ride alongside code and sync via Git.</span><span class='zh'>Git-bug 是一个嵌入在 Git 中的分布式离线优先缺陷跟踪器，使问题跟踪与代码同处，并通过 Git 同步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Git-bug is a distributed, offline-first bug tracker embedded in Git, letting issues ride alongside code and sync via Git. It minimizes external dependencies and can improve collaboration in resource-constrained or disconnected environments.</span><span class='zh'>Git-bug 是一个嵌入在 Git 中的分布式离线优先缺陷跟踪器，使问题跟踪与代码同处，并通过 Git 同步。它减少对外部服务的依赖，适合受限网络或离线环境的协作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ntfargo/Relapse-Exploit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PS5 Relapse Exploit</span><span class='zh'>PS5 Relapse 漏洞利用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Relapse appears to be a PS5 vulnerability exploit that enables arbitrary code execution on affected firmware, potentially allowing homebrew or custom firmware.</span><span class='zh'>Relapse 漏洞利用工具可能在受影响的固件上实现任意代码执行，或允许自制固件和自制程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Relapse appears to be a PS5 vulnerability exploit that enables arbitrary code execution on affected firmware, potentially allowing homebrew or custom firmware. The discovery underscores persistent security risks in console ecosystems and the ongoing race between protection and exploitation.</span><span class='zh'>Relapse 漏洞利用工具可能在受影响的固件上实现任意代码执行，或允许自制固件和自制程序。此发现再次凸显游戏主机生态系统的长期安全风险，以及防护与利用之间的博弈。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/09/29/top_stories_09292026'><span class='en'>Sep 29</span><span class='zh'>9月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>336</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49895304' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>226</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49750152" data-id="49750152" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="331" data-comments="94" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49750152" data-time="1789705224">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jemalloc/jemalloc/releases/tag/5.4.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jemalloc 5.4.0</span><span class='zh'>jemalloc 5.4.0 发布与改进</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The 5.4.0 release brings memory allocator improvements, including better fragmentation handling, new tuning knobs, and bug fixes that boost throughput in multi-core workloads.</span><span class='zh'>5.4.0 版本在内存分配器上带来改进，包括更好的碎片处理、新增调优选项和若干 bug 修复，提升多核负载下的吞吐和延迟表现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The 5.4.0 release brings memory allocator improvements, including better fragmentation handling, new tuning knobs, and bug fixes that boost throughput in multi-core workloads. It’s a solid upgrade for latency-sensitive services and large-scale systems.</span><span class='zh'>5.4.0 版本在内存分配器上带来改进，包括更好的碎片处理、新增调优选项和若干 bug 修复，提升多核负载下的吞吐和延迟表现。对于对延迟敏感的服务和大型系统而言，这是一个值得关注的升级。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PhiloLabs/fable51-worlds" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fable 5.1 World Modeling</span><span class='zh'>Fable 5.1 的世界建模</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fable 5.1 introduces world modeling capabilities, enabling agents to build internal representations of their environments and simulate outcomes for planning.</span><span class='zh'>Fable 5.1 引入世界建模能力，使代理能够构建环境的内部表示并对结果进行仿真以便规划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fable 5.1 introduces world modeling capabilities, enabling agents to build internal representations of their environments and simulate outcomes for planning. It highlights modular &#x27;worlds&#x27; that support scalable reasoning and generalization across tasks. For developers, this signals a shift toward more sample-efficient, model-based approaches in interactive AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Fable 5.1 引入世界建模能力，使代理能够构建环境的内部表示并对结果进行仿真以便规划。它强调支持跨任务的可扩展推理与通用化的模块化“世界”概念。对开发者而言，标志着向更高效、基于模型的交互式 AI 系统迈进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GrapheneOS/Messaging/releases/tag/13" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS&#x27; rewritten Messages app is released</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 发布重写版 Messages 应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS has released a rewritten Messages app built for privacy-first Android environments, removing reliance on Google&#x27;s default messaging stack.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 已发布面向隐私优先 Android 环境的重写 Messages 应用，摆脱对 Google 默认短信栈的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS has released a rewritten Messages app built for privacy-first Android environments, removing reliance on Google&#x27;s default messaging stack. The move shows how privacy-focused ROMs pursue hardened, open-source messaging with end-to-end security. It&#x27;s a win for users seeking independent, privacy-preserving messaging on mobile.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 已发布面向隐私优先 Android 环境的重写 Messages 应用，摆脱对 Google 默认短信栈的依赖。此举凸显专注隐私的 ROM 如何通过开源实现更安全的端到端消息传输。对于寻求独立、透明隐私保护的移动用户而言，这是一个值得关注的进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/argonautlabsai/deltafin" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3 (2.8T) at 1 token/s on a MacBook Pro, streamed from four SSDs</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3（2.8T）在 MacBook Pro 上以每秒 1 個 token，來自四個 SSD 的流式推理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Kimi K3 (2.8T) runs at 1 token per second on a MacBook Pro, streaming from four SSDs.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3（2.8T）在 MacBook Pro 上以每秒 1 個 token 的速度推理，數據由四個 SSD 流式提供。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Kimi K3 (2.8T) runs at 1 token per second on a MacBook Pro, streaming from four SSDs. This demonstrates how high-throughput storage and portable hardware can sustain on-device inference for large models, signaling practical paths for mobile AI experiments.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3（2.8T）在 MacBook Pro 上以每秒 1 個 token 的速度推理，數據由四個 SSD 流式提供。此展現顯示高帶寬存儲與便攜硬件可支撐大模型的本地推理，為移動端 AI 實驗提供實用路徑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/volotat/mini-AGI/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mini-AGI – Dynamic continual learning model trained on 8GB VRAM</span><span class='zh'>Mini-AGI：在 8GB 显存上从零开始训练的动态持续学习模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mini-AGI demonstrates a dynamic continual learning model trained from scratch on 8GB VRAM, highlighting progress toward lightweight, adaptable AI on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Mini-AGI 展示了一个在 8GB 显存上从零开始训练的动态持续学习模型，凸显了在入门级硬件上实现轻量化、可适应的 AI 的进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mini-AGI demonstrates a dynamic continual learning model trained from scratch on 8GB VRAM, highlighting progress toward lightweight, adaptable AI on consumer hardware. The project aims to show how small GPUs can sustain learning across tasks without full retraining.</span><span class='zh'>Mini-AGI 展示了一个在 8GB 显存上从零开始训练的动态持续学习模型，凸显了在入门级硬件上实现轻量化、可适应的 AI 的进展。该项目旨在展示小型 GPU 如何在不同任务间持续学习、而无需完全重新训练。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zedeus/nitter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Following legal advice, the Nitter project will continue</span><span class='zh'>在法律意见指引下，Nitter 项目将继续</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nitter&#x27;s maintainers say they will continue developing the privacy-respecting Twitter front-end after receiving legal counsel.</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 的维护者表示在获得法律意见后将继续开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nitter&#x27;s maintainers say they will continue developing the privacy-respecting Twitter front-end after receiving legal counsel. The move highlights ongoing tension between open-source Twitter front-ends and platform policies, and how developers navigate compliance.</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 的维护者表示在获得法律意见后将继续开发。此举凸显开源 Twitter 前端与平台政策之间持续的紧张关系，以及开发者在合规性方面的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/security.txt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HuggingFace: Security.txt</span><span class='zh'>HuggingFace 的 security.txt</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HuggingFace adopts the security.txt standard to publish security contact details and disclosure policy, making it easier for researchers to report vulnerabilities.</span><span class='zh'>HuggingFace 采用 security.txt 标准，公开安全联系信息和披露策略，方便研究人员报告漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HuggingFace adopts the security.txt standard to publish security contact details and disclosure policy, making it easier for researchers to report vulnerabilities. This aligns with industry best practices and helps shorten response times while boosting security posture.</span><span class='zh'>HuggingFace 采用 security.txt 标准，公开安全联系信息和披露策略，方便研究人员报告漏洞。此举符合行业最佳实践，提升响应速度与安全治理水平。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PostHog/jeeves" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jeeves. Reasoning improves Jev-like decision models</span><span class='zh'>Jeeves：推理能力提升 Jev 风格决策模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jeeves introduces explicit reasoning to Jev-like decision models, boosting reliability and interpretability.</span><span class='zh'>Jeeves 将显式推理引入 Jev 风格的决策模型，提升可靠性与可解释性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jeeves introduces explicit reasoning to Jev-like decision models, boosting reliability and interpretability. The project experiments with integrating step-by-step logic into AI-assisted analytics to improve decision quality.</span><span class='zh'>Jeeves 将显式推理引入 Jev 风格的决策模型，提升可靠性与可解释性。该项目尝试将逐步推理逻辑嵌入 AI 辅助分析中，以提高决策质量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop/security/advisories/GHSA-7hp8-65ch-5whp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WordPress: Unauthenticated path traversal leading to conditional RCE</span><span class='zh'>WordPress：未认证路径遍历引发条件性远程代码执行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An unauthenticated path traversal flaw in WordPress can lead to conditional remote code execution under specific setups.</span><span class='zh'>存在未认证的路径遍历漏洞，特定条件下可实现条件性远程代码执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An unauthenticated path traversal flaw in WordPress can lead to conditional remote code execution under specific setups. Remediation requires applying patched versions and hardening the environment against attacker-controlled file paths.</span><span class='zh'>存在未认证的路径遍历漏洞，特定条件下可实现条件性远程代码执行。修复需升级到修补版本并加强对攻击者可控文件路径的防护。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8 adds reasoning-prefill capabilities modeled after GPT-5.5 Pro, aiming to streamline common thought patterns in prompts.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8 增加了基于 GPT-5.5 Pro 的推理预填充，意在简化提示中的常见推理模式。</span></p>

This could improve consistency and speed in complex reasoning tasks for developers and researchers.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8 增加了基于 GPT-5.5 Pro 的推理预填充，意在简化提示中的常见推理模式。此举可能提升复杂推理任务的一致性与速度，对开发者和研究人员有影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xCuri0/ReBarUEFI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ReBarUEFI: Resizable BAR for almost any UEFI system</span><span class='zh'>ReBarUEFI：几乎所有 UEFI 系统的可伸缩 BAR 支持</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ReBarUEFI brings Resizable BAR support to a broad swath of UEFI systems, enabling dynamic GPU memory mappings without changing hardware.</span><span class='zh'>ReBarUEFI 让大量被支持的 UEFI 系统具备可伸缩显存条功能，提升显存映射的灵活性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ReBarUEFI brings Resizable BAR support to a broad swath of UEFI systems, enabling dynamic GPU memory mappings without changing hardware. By enabling BAR resizing at firmware level, it broadens compatibility and could unlock modest performance gains for enthusiasts on older platforms. It&#x27;s a reminder that firmware tinkering can squeeze extra life from existing rigs.</span><span class='zh'>ReBarUEFI 让大量被支持的 UEFI 系统具备可伸缩显存条功能，提升显存映射的灵活性。通过固件层面的改造实现 BAR 大小调节，扩大对旧平台的兼容性，并有望带来一定的性能提升。提醒人们对固件层优化也能挖掘硬件潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/carloslfu/slotstream" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Running 104GB Qwen3.8-Flash-Next on 48GB Mac with at ~12 tok/s</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 48GB Mac 上以约每秒 12 token 运行 104GB 的 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 104GB Qwen3.8-Flash-Next model runs on a 48GB Mac with runtime around 12 tokens per second, enabled by memory-efficient offloading and optimizations.</span><span class='zh'>演示在 48GB 内存的 Mac 上以约每秒 12 个 token 运行 104GB 的 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next，靠内存高效的 offloading 与优化技巧实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 104GB Qwen3.8-Flash-Next model runs on a 48GB Mac with runtime around 12 tokens per second, enabled by memory-efficient offloading and optimizations. The post demonstrates how large models can be squeezed onto consumer hardware with careful engineering. It hints at a future where hobbyists can experiment with very large models on commodity machines.</span><span class='zh'>演示在 48GB 内存的 Mac 上以约每秒 12 个 token 运行 104GB 的 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next，靠内存高效的 offloading 与优化技巧实现。该展示表明通过精心设计，甚至大型模型也可在普通硬件上运行。预示着爱好者有望在性价比机器上试验超大模型的未来。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maanHimself/OpenDLSS-NR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS: A Vulkan Reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 Neural Rendering Network</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR：基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs. If successful, it could empower developers to integrate neural upscaling into Vulkan apps without relying on Nvidia&#x27;s stack, but performance and fidelity benchmarks remain uncertain.</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。若成熟可让开发者在 Vulkan 应用中实现神经上采样，而不必依赖 Nvidia 的专有栈，但性能和画质的基准仍在发展中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/pull/21327" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>uv: Deduplicate all files in the wheel cache</span><span class='zh'>uv：对 wheel 缓存中的所有文件进行去重</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A PR proposes deduplicating all files in Python&#x27;s wheel cache, saving disk space and reducing redundancy.</span><span class='zh'>提交的 PR 提案对 wheel 缓存中的所有文件进行去重，节省磁盘空间、减少冗余。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A PR proposes deduplicating all files in Python&#x27;s wheel cache, saving disk space and reducing redundancy. The change helps environments with large dependency trees run more predictably.</span><span class='zh'>提交的 PR 提案对 wheel 缓存中的所有文件进行去重，节省磁盘空间、减少冗余。此举有助于大依赖环境下的可预测性与稳定性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rh1tech/frank-386" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 386 PC for Your RP2350</span><span class='zh'>在 RP2350 上的 386 PC</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 386 PC for Your RP2350 shows a project that emulates a 386-era PC on the RP2350 platform, enabling retro software and educational demos on a microcontroller-board.</span><span class='zh'>这个在 RP2350 上实现的 386 电脑项目，可以在微控制器板上运行 386 时代的软件，适合教学演示和复古计算爱好者实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 386 PC for Your RP2350 shows a project that emulates a 386-era PC on the RP2350 platform, enabling retro software and educational demos on a microcontroller-board. It demonstrates how hobbyists squeeze legacy computing into tiny devices and what it implies for embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>这个在 RP2350 上实现的 386 电脑项目，可以在微控制器板上运行 386 时代的软件，适合教学演示和复古计算爱好者实验。展示了爱好者如何把遗留计算嵌入到小型设备中，并对嵌入式系统的潜力提供了启示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/enactic/OpenArm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenArm: An open-source 7DOF humanoid arm</span><span class='zh'>OpenArm：一个开源的七自由度机器人臂</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenArm is an open-source seven-DOF robotic arm project with modular hardware and ROS-ready software.</span><span class='zh'>OpenArm 是一个开源的七自由度机器人手臂项目，提供模块化硬件和面向 ROS 的软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenArm is an open-source seven-DOF robotic arm project with modular hardware and ROS-ready software. It lowers barriers for researchers and hobbyists to experiment with high-DOF control, prototyping, and robotics education.</span><span class='zh'>OpenArm 是一个开源的七自由度机器人手臂项目，提供模块化硬件和面向 ROS 的软件。它降低了研究者和爱好者在高自由度机器人控制、快速原型开发与教育方面的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gioblu/NPC-Forge/blob/main/docs/development.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TERMy – A fast terminal assistant that does not use LLMs</span><span class='zh'>TERMy：一个不使用大模型的快速终端助手</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TERMy is a fast terminal assistant that runs without LLMs, trading off cloud AI for local processing and speed.</span><span class='zh'>TERMy 是一个不依赖大模型的快速终端助手，改以本地处理取代云端 AI，追求更快的响应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TERMy is a fast terminal assistant that runs without LLMs, trading off cloud AI for local processing and speed. It demonstrates a rising interest in lightweight, privacy-preserving tooling that can operate offline and with minimal latency.</span><span class='zh'>TERMy 是一个不依赖大模型的快速终端助手，改以本地处理取代云端 AI，追求更快的响应。它体现了对轻量级、注重隐私的工具的日益关注，尤其是能离线工作且延迟更低。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/magnitudedev/magnitude" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – Self-optimizing inference engine for agents</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – 面向代理的自我优化推理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency. It sits at the intersection of AI agent frameworks and efficient inference, promising to reduce manual tuning and hardware waste. Backed by YC S25, it signals growing interest in tooling for autonomous agents.</span><span class='zh'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。它处在 AI 代理框架与高效推理技术的交汇处，代表了为自走代理提升算力利用率的一条新路径。由 YC S25 支持，反映了对自动化代理工具的热切关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GraafHenk/numberwang" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WangNet – 1.8 MB, zero-dependency Numberwang adjudication in 11 languages</span><span class='zh'>WangNet——1.8 MB、零依赖的 Numberwang 判定，覆盖 11 种语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WangNet is a compact, 1.8 MB, zero-dependency model for Numberwang adjudication available in 11 languages.</span><span class='zh'>WangNet 是一个 1.8MB、零依赖的 Numberwang 判定模型，支持 11 种语言。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WangNet is a compact, 1.8 MB, zero-dependency model for Numberwang adjudication available in 11 languages. The project demonstrates how tiny, self-contained ML tooling can perform lightweight linguistic tasks offline, without external dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>WangNet 是一个 1.8MB、零依赖的 Numberwang 判定模型，支持 11 种语言。该项目展示了体积极小、可离线运行的机器学习工具在语言任务上的可用性，以及对依赖的剥离所带来的便携性好处。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kennethwolters/litelm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Litelm: LiteLLM Without the Bloat</span><span class='zh'>Litelm：去臃肿的轻量级 LLM 实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Litelm presents a compact LLM toolkit aimed at reducing dependencies and memory footprint for edge use.</span><span class='zh'>Litelm 提供一个紧凑的 LLM 工具包，目标是降低依赖与内存占用，便于在边缘设备上使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Litelm presents a compact LLM toolkit aimed at reducing dependencies and memory footprint for edge use. It targets speed and simplicity, enabling experiments with smaller models without heavyweight infrastructure. The project underscores a trend toward accessible, privacy-friendly AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Litelm 提供一个紧凑的 LLM 工具包，目标是降低依赖与内存占用，便于在边缘设备上使用。它强调速度与简洁性，支持在不依赖繁重基础设施的情况下尝试小型模型。该项目凸显向更易获取且注重隐私的 AI 工具的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kyle-pena-nlp/jevchat/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I turned Jev into a (lousy) chatbot</span><span class='zh'>我把 Jev 系统改造成了一个（糟糕的）聊天机器人</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An account of turning the Jev system into a chatbot with disappointing results, illustrating the gap between theory and practice in building conversational AI.</span><span class='zh'>作者记录了将 Jev 系统改造成聊天机器人的尝试，结果表现不佳且存在明显局限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An account of turning the Jev system into a chatbot with disappointing results, illustrating the gap between theory and practice in building conversational AI. It spotlights data quality, alignment, and evaluation challenges that plague many hobbyist or lightweight chatbot efforts.</span><span class='zh'>作者记录了将 Jev 系统改造成聊天机器人的尝试，结果表现不佳且存在明显局限。文章强调在数据、对齐与安全性不足时构建对话式 AI 的实际陷阱，以及在现实场景中实现可用性所面临的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Speedstu/CUDA-for-AMD-Windows" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CUDA for AMD on Windows</span><span class='zh'>Windows 上的 AMD CUDA 实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community project brings CUDA-like tooling to AMD GPUs on Windows, enabling CUDA-dependent workflows to run on non-NVIDIA hardware.</span><span class='zh'>社区项目让 AMD 显卡在 Windows 上获得类似 CUDA 的体验，扩展了不能使用 NVIDIA 的硬件场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community project brings CUDA-like tooling to AMD GPUs on Windows, enabling CUDA-dependent workflows to run on non-NVIDIA hardware. While not a drop-in replacement, it broadens hardware choices for ML developers and hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>社区项目让 AMD 显卡在 Windows 上获得类似 CUDA 的体验，扩展了不能使用 NVIDIA 的硬件场景。尽管不是完全替代，但为 ML 开发者和爱好者带来更广的硬件选择。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Sadpainy/Stuxnet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Stuxnet – A reconstructed source code of the infamous cyber-weapon</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Stuxnet——臭名昭著网络武器的重建源码</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A portion of Stuxnet&#x27;s source code has been reconstructed and published on GitHub, offering researchers a rare peek into the malware&#x27;s inner workings.</span><span class='zh'>公开了一部分 Stuxnet 的重建源码，研究者可以通过该代码更深入理解这起臭名昭著的网络武器的实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A portion of Stuxnet&#x27;s source code has been reconstructed and published on GitHub, offering researchers a rare peek into the malware&#x27;s inner workings. The release illuminates the malware&#x27;s modular design, multi-stage propagation, and PLC targeting that made it historically sophisticated. For security teams, it serves as a concrete reference point for building defenses and understanding exploit chains.</span><span class='zh'>公开了一部分 Stuxnet 的重建源码，研究者可以通过该代码更深入理解这起臭名昭著的网络武器的实现。发布揭示了该木马的模块化设计、分阶段传播及对 PLC 的定向攻击等要点，为防御研究提供了参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dashersw/coyopedal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Guitar amp and effects pedal built on the Waveshare ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-2.06</span><span class='zh'>基于 Waveshare ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-2.06 的吉他放大器与效果器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

It demonstrates hardware hacking, real-time audio processing, and DIY creativity.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用 ESP32-S3 和触控显示屏打造吉他放大器与效果器的动手项目，展示了低成本微控制器如何实现便携、可定制的音频设备。项目融合硬件黑客、实时音频处理与创意实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nestrilabs/virtio-nvgpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Virtio-nvgpu: Near-native Nvidia GPU access inside a KVM guest</span><span class='zh'>Virtio-nvgpu：在 KVM 来宾中实现近原生 Nvidia GPU 访问</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Virtio-nvgpu enables near-native Nvidia GPU access from within a KVM guest, enabling GPU-accelerated workloads without full PCIe passthrough.</span><span class='zh'>Virtio-nvgpu 让 KVM 来宾中实现近原生 Nvidia GPU 访问，使得在不进行完整 PCIe 直通的情况下也能运行 GPU 加速的工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Virtio-nvgpu enables near-native Nvidia GPU access from within a KVM guest, enabling GPU-accelerated workloads without full PCIe passthrough. This could simplify multi-tenant GPU usage and improve VM performance for ML workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Virtio-nvgpu 让 KVM 来宾中实现近原生 Nvidia GPU 访问，使得在不进行完整 PCIe 直通的情况下也能运行 GPU 加速的工作负载。它有望简化多租户 GPU 使用并提升机器学习工作负载的虚拟机性能。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An ESP32-S3 cluster runs a 1.58-bit BitNet language model, pushing edge AI further into tiny hardware.</span><span class='zh'>ESP32S3 集群在边缘硬件上运行一个 1.58 位量化的 BitNet 语言模型，推动边缘AI走向更小的硬件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An ESP32-S3 cluster runs a 1.58-bit BitNet language model, pushing edge AI further into tiny hardware. The demo showcases continued progress in running language models on embedded devices, raising questions about practicality, latency, and use cases.</span><span class='zh'>ESP32S3 集群在边缘硬件上运行一个 1.58 位量化的 BitNet 语言模型，推动边缘AI走向更小的硬件。该演示展示在嵌入式设备上运行语言模型的持续进展，同时引发关于可用性、延迟和小型模型训练的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PaperMono is an e-ink fridge magnet that syncs with a mobile web page for editing your shopping list, enabling a low-power, glanceable workflow. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community project released a byte-identical decompilation of the GameCube version of Resident Evil 4 into C/C++, showcasing advances in reverse engineering and preservation. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WaveDigger analyzes wireless signals to infer physical locations, using signal fingerprints and ML for localization without GPS. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gauravapiscean/agentic-kv-cache" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LRU is harder to beat than the KV-cache papers suggest</span><span class='zh'>LRU比KV缓存论文所示的更难被打败</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New measurements suggest that LRU remains a robust baseline under realistic workloads, harder to outperform than some KV-cache proposals imply.</span><span class='zh'>新实验表明，在真实工作负载下，最近提出的KV缓存方案并不总能超越LRU，LRU的表现反而更稳健。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New measurements suggest that LRU remains a robust baseline under realistic workloads, harder to outperform than some KV-cache proposals imply. The finding has practical implications for how systems architects choose cache strategies and benchmark performance. It reinforces the value of well-understood, simple policies.</span><span class='zh'>新实验表明，在真实工作负载下，最近提出的KV缓存方案并不总能超越LRU，LRU的表现反而更稳健。此发现影响系统架构在缓存策略与性能基准上的取舍，强化了简单、易于理解的策略的价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TauricResearch/TradingAgents" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Multi-Agents LLM Financial Trading Framework</span><span class='zh'>多代理LLM金融交易框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A modular framework enables multiple LLM agents to coordinate trading decisions, potentially expanding automated strategies beyond single-model limits.</span><span class='zh'>一个模块化框架允许多名LLM代理协同进行交易决策，理论上扩展了自动化策略的边界。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A modular framework enables multiple LLM agents to coordinate trading decisions, potentially expanding automated strategies beyond single-model limits. It unlocks speed and diversification but also raises risk management, reproducibility, and regulatory considerations.</span><span class='zh'>一个模块化框架允许多名LLM代理协同进行交易决策，理论上扩展了自动化策略的边界。它带来更高的速度与策略多样性，同时也带来风险管理、可重复性与监管方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lfarroco/ogre-battle-64-recomp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ogre Battle 64 Recompiled Project at 99.05%</span><span class='zh'>Ogre Battle 64 重编译项目达到 99.05%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ogre Battle 64 Recompiled Project reports progress toward 99.05% compatibility, a milestone for reverse-engineering and emulation tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Ogre Battle 64 的重编译/逆向工程项目接近完成，兼容性达到 99.05%，有望提升性能并便利模组开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ogre Battle 64 Recompiled Project reports progress toward 99.05% compatibility, a milestone for reverse-engineering and emulation tooling. The achievement highlights community-driven preservation and the potential for easier modding, debugging, and performance gains on classic titles.</span><span class='zh'>Ogre Battle 64 的重编译/逆向工程项目接近完成，兼容性达到 99.05%，有望提升性能并便利模组开发。这体现了社区在经典游戏保存与工具链改进方面的持续努力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/marsbos/mador" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mador – Make any DOM reactive with a tiny 80-line Proxy state tuple</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Mador——用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组让任意 DOM 实现响应式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mador is a tiny library that makes any DOM reactive using an 80-line Proxy-based state tuple.</span><span class='zh'>Mador 是一个极简库，使用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组即可让任意 DOM 具备响应式能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mador is a tiny library that makes any DOM reactive using an 80-line Proxy-based state tuple. It demonstrates that practical reactivity can be achieved with a tiny footprint, offering a minimal entry point into how reactive systems work. A useful reference for experimenting with lean, framework-free UI design.</span><span class='zh'>Mador 是一个极简库，使用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组即可让任意 DOM 具备响应式能力。它展示了以极小的体积实现响应式的可能性，为理解响应式编程提供了简练的入门范例。对于探索无框架、精简 UI 设计的开发者颇具参考价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepfates/imp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems. The project enables BEAM friendly probabilistic programming workflows and leverages the actor model for parallelism across cores.</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。该项目为 BEAM 上的概率编程和并行工作流提供更友好的实现，利用 actor 模型在多核上实现并行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/awlevin/typesafe-computer-use" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Typesafe-computer-use drives a Mac toward a goal for 1/50th of a cent per step</span><span class='zh'>类型安全的用机：让 Mac 的每一步成本降至 0.02 美分</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A quirky project shows that type safe programming and disciplined computer use can shave tiny costs per operation on a Mac, aiming for about 0.02 cents per step.</span><span class='zh'>一个离奇的项目展示通过严格的类型安全编程和自律的使用习惯，可以将 Mac 上每一步的成本降至约 0.02 美分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A quirky project shows that type safe programming and disciplined computer use can shave tiny costs per operation on a Mac, aiming for about 0.02 cents per step. It demonstrates how micro optimizations, tooling choices, and principled coding can accumulate into meaningful savings over time in everyday computing tasks.</span><span class='zh'>一个离奇的项目展示通过严格的类型安全编程和自律的使用习惯，可以将 Mac 上每一步的成本降至约 0.02 美分。该研究揭示了微观优化、工具选择和规范化编码如何在长期日常计算中叠加出可观的节省。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Sparticle62ops/pssa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PSSA: A non-transformer language model written from scratch in Rust</span><span class='zh'>PSSA：用 Rust 从零实现的非 Transformer 语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PSSA is a Rust-written, non-transformer language model that showcases what a lean architecture can look like.</span><span class='zh'>PSSA 是一个用 Rust 写成的非 Transformer 语言模型，展示了简约架构的可能样貌。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PSSA is a Rust-written, non-transformer language model that showcases what a lean architecture can look like. It raises questions about performance, training data, and when transformers are truly necessary.</span><span class='zh'>PSSA 是一个用 Rust 写成的非 Transformer 语言模型，展示了简约架构的可能样貌。它引发关于性能、训练数据以及何时必须使用 Transformer 的思考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/trycua/cua" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: CUA-S1 – A System One Model for Computer Use</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：CUA-S1——面向计算机使用的 System One 模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN introduces CUA-S1, a System One model aimed at computer use, showcasing a lightweight, modular approach to an AI assistant that helps with everyday computing tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了 CUA-S1，这是一个面向计算机使用的 System One 模型，采用轻量化、模块化设计的 AI 助手来协助日常计算任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN introduces CUA-S1, a System One model aimed at computer use, showcasing a lightweight, modular approach to an AI assistant that helps with everyday computing tasks. It invites hands-on experimentation and highlights practical AI tooling for real workflows. Takeaway: small, user-focused AI systems can meaningfully augment daily computer use.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了 CUA-S1，这是一个面向计算机使用的 System One 模型，采用轻量化、模块化设计的 AI 助手来协助日常计算任务。文章强调了可操作性和实际工作流中的工具化应用，鼓励社区参与。要点在于小型、以用户为中心的 AI 系统能够切实提升日常计算体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/apple/LensVLM-9B" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LensVLM: Compressing long context as images, expanding only relevant pages</span><span class='zh'>LensVLM：将长上下文压缩为图像，仅展开相关页面</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LensVLM encodes long-context documents as compact images to fit memory, then expands only the relevant pages during query-time retrieval.</span><span class='zh'>LensVLM 将长上下文文档压缩成图像以节省显存，再在检索阶段仅展开与查询相关的页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LensVLM encodes long-context documents as compact images to fit memory, then expands only the relevant pages during query-time retrieval. The approach lowers memory bandwidth needs while preserving access to long-form information for multimodal LLMs.</span><span class='zh'>LensVLM 将长上下文文档压缩成图像以节省显存，再在检索阶段仅展开与查询相关的页面。该方法降低了显存带宽需求，同时为多模态语言模型保留了访问长文本的能力。</span></p>

For developers and players, it highlights how specialized AI skills can streamline study workflows.</span><span class='zh'>该 Claude Code 技能通过解析 PGN 数据分析棋局，提供关键失误、最佳走法和局面策略的洞见，展示 AI 辅助工具在特定领域分析中的应用潜力。对开发者和棋手而言，这类专业技能有助于提升学习与研究的效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paradise-runner/toast" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Toast, a beautiful by default in terminal IDE</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：默认美观的终端 IDE</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Toast is a terminal-based IDE designed with sensible defaults to minimize setup friction and maximize focus.</span><span class='zh'>Toast 是一个以默认设置友好、追求高效聚焦的终端 IDE。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Toast is a terminal-based IDE designed with sensible defaults to minimize setup friction and maximize focus. It emphasizes a clean, fast UX with editor features typical of GUI IDEs, but in a lightweight terminal form. The project demonstrates how developers are rethinking IDE ergonomics for terminal workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Toast 是一个以默认设置友好、追求高效聚焦的终端 IDE。它在终端形态中保留了常见的编辑器功能，强调简洁快速的用户体验，同时降低上手门槛。该项目展示了开发者如何在终端工作流中重新思考 IDE 的人体工学。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kadō is an open-source iOS habit tracker that uses a non-binary habit score rather than a simple done/not-done metric.</span><span class='zh'>Kadō 是一款开源的 iOS 习惯追踪应用，采用非二元化的习惯评分而非简单完成/未完成的二元指标。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Walgit proposes a Git server architecture where a single binary fronts an object store, potentially simplifying deployment and scaling.</span><span class='zh'>Walgit 提出一种将对象存储作为后端、仅用一个二进制前端的 Git 服务器架构，便于部署和扩展，同时也引发对性能和存储一致性的思考。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Walgit proposes a Git server architecture where a single binary fronts an object store, potentially simplifying deployment and scaling. The design raises questions about performance, consistency models, and storage backend choices.</span><span class='zh'>Walgit 提出一种将对象存储作为后端、仅用一个二进制前端的 Git 服务器架构，便于部署和扩展，同时也引发对性能和存储一致性的思考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/okf-memory/okf-agent-memory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OKF Agent Memory – Git-native persistent memory for AI coding agents</span><span class='zh'>OKF Agent Memory：面向 AI 编码代理的 Git 原生持久内存</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OKF Agent Memory provides git-native persistent memory for AI coding agents, allowing state to persist across sessions and improving reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>OKF Agent Memory 为 AI 编码代理提供 Git 原生的持久内存，使状态能在跨会话间持久化，提升可重复性和调试效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OKF Agent Memory provides git-native persistent memory for AI coding agents, allowing state to persist across sessions and improving reproducibility. The approach integrates versioned state management with agent workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OKF Agent Memory 为 AI 编码代理提供 Git 原生的持久内存，使状态能在跨会话间持久化，提升可重复性和调试效率。该方案将版本化状态管理与代理工作流结合起来。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nefarius/vd_agent/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I refuse to let SPICE die</span><span class='zh'>我不让SPICE死去</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SPICE—the long‑standing circuit simulator—remains active thanks to community efforts and forks that keep it compatible with modern tooling.</span><span class='zh'>依然活跃的SPICE电路仿真工具，得益于社区努力和分支保持与现代工具链的兼容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SPICE—the long‑standing circuit simulator—remains active thanks to community efforts and forks that keep it compatible with modern tooling. Ongoing work blends older methodologies with newer workflows, preserving an essential tool for education and research in electronics. The piece is a tribute to sustaining open‑source engineering tools.</span><span class='zh'>依然活跃的SPICE电路仿真工具，得益于社区努力和分支保持与现代工具链的兼容。持续的工作在保留传统方法的同时融入新型工作流，为电子学教育和研究保留了一项不可或缺的开源工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hsfzxjy/npunlock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Npunlock – Run custom C kernels for Intel NPUs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Npunlock——在 Intel NPU 上运行自定义 C 内核</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Npunlock is a Show HN project that lets developers run custom C kernels on Intel NPUs, enabling experimentation outside the official SDKs.</span><span class='zh'>Npunlock 是一个 Show HN 项目，允许在英特尔 NPU 上运行自定义 C 内核，便于绕过官方 SDK 进行实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Npunlock is a Show HN project that lets developers run custom C kernels on Intel NPUs, enabling experimentation outside the official SDKs. It highlights both potential performance gains and concerns around stability, security, and vendor restrictions.</span><span class='zh'>Npunlock 是一个 Show HN 项目，允许在英特尔 NPU 上运行自定义 C 内核，便于绕过官方 SDK 进行实验。它既展示了开放性带来的潜在性能收益，也引发关于稳定性、安全性和厂商限制的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pkimpel/retro-1620" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Web-based IBM 1620 emulator and IPL-V from 1963</span><span class='zh'>基于网络的 IBM 1620 仿真器与 IPL-V 1963</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A web-based IBM 1620 emulator and IPL-V interpreter lets enthusiasts explore historic computing in the browser, preserving legacy software and teaching computing history.</span><span class='zh'>一个可在浏览器中运行的 IBM 1620 仿真器及 IPL-V 解释器，使爱好者能够在现代环境中体验历史计算，帮助保存遗留软件并传授计算机史。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A web-based IBM 1620 emulator and IPL-V interpreter lets enthusiasts explore historic computing in the browser, preserving legacy software and teaching computing history. The project highlights how emulation lowers barriers to experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>一个可在浏览器中运行的 IBM 1620 仿真器及 IPL-V 解释器，使爱好者能够在现代环境中体验历史计算，帮助保存遗留软件并传授计算机史。仿真技术降低了学习与实验的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Avinash-jetwani/jevmem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jevmem – automatic project memory for Claude Code, built on Jev</span><span class='zh'>Jevmem——基于 Jev 的 Claude Code 自动项目记忆</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jevmem provides automatic project memory for Claude Code by leveraging Jev, enabling persistent context across coding sessions.</span><span class='zh'>Jevmem利用Jev为Claude Code提供自动化的项目记忆能力，使跨会话的上下文可持续保留。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jevmem provides automatic project memory for Claude Code by leveraging Jev, enabling persistent context across coding sessions. This reduces repetitive setup, boosts productivity, and makes it easier to manage long-running AI-assisted projects.</span><span class='zh'>Jevmem利用Jev为Claude Code提供自动化的项目记忆能力，使跨会话的上下文可持续保留。减少重复设置、提高生产力，并帮助管理大型AI辅助项目的进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/timgordontg/engrim" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Engrim – A universal, local-first SQLite memory engine for AI CLIs</span><span class='zh'>Engrim：用于 AI 命令行界面的本地优先 SQLite 内存引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Engrim is a universal, local-first SQLite memory engine for AI CLIs, enabling offline-friendly memory and state management for AI-powered CLIs.</span><span class='zh'>Engrim 是一个通用的本地优先 SQLite 内存引擎，面向 AI 命令行界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Engrim is a universal, local-first SQLite memory engine for AI CLIs, enabling offline-friendly memory and state management for AI-powered CLIs. It promises consistency, portability, and data sovereignty for local AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Engrim 是一个通用的本地优先 SQLite 内存引擎，面向 AI 命令行界面。它为本地 AI 工作流提供离线记忆和状态管理，强调一致性、可移植性和数据主权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/HexLasso/FF-16-TUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Interactive pattern discovery in binaries (FF-16-TUI)</span><span class='zh'>FF-16-TUI：二进制中的交互式模式发现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FF-16-TUI introduces a terminal-based interface for interactive pattern discovery in binaries, lowering the barrier for reverse engineers and security researchers to explore patterns without heavy GUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>FF-16-TUI 提供一个基于终端的交互界面，用于在二进制文件中发现模式，降低了逆向分析和安全研究的门槛，便于在无需重型 GUI 的情况下进行探索性工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FF-16-TUI introduces a terminal-based interface for interactive pattern discovery in binaries, lowering the barrier for reverse engineers and security researchers to explore patterns without heavy GUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>FF-16-TUI 提供一个基于终端的交互界面，用于在二进制文件中发现模式，降低了逆向分析和安全研究的门槛，便于在无需重型 GUI 的情况下进行探索性工作。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bug report notes that an unusual interaction with border-radius styling affects the VSCode editor rendering, illustrating how UI theming can leak into the code editor.</span><span class='zh'>关于边框圆角样式意外影响 VSCode 编辑器渲染的错误报告，揭示了 UI 主题对代码编辑器可能的渗透与脆弱性，强调开发者工具中主题设计的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bug report notes that an unusual interaction with border-radius styling affects the VSCode editor rendering, illustrating how UI theming can leak into the code editor. It underscores the fragility of layered UI and the importance of robust theming in developer tools.</span><span class='zh'>关于边框圆角样式意外影响 VSCode 编辑器渲染的错误报告，揭示了 UI 主题对代码编辑器可能的渗透与脆弱性，强调开发者工具中主题设计的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openlayer-ai/jevals" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: jevals – replacing LLM judges with typed Jev decisions</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：jevals——用类型化的 Jev 判定替代 LLM 评测</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>jevals is a show-and-tell project replacing flaky LLM judges with typed, deterministic judgments, aiming for reproducible evaluations.</span><span class='zh'>jevals 是一个展示性项目，用类型化、确定性的判定来取代易出错的 LLM 评审，目标是实现可重复的评估。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>jevals is a show-and-tell project replacing flaky LLM judges with typed, deterministic judgments, aiming for reproducible evaluations. It shifts evaluation away from soft, adapter-based prompts toward stricter, verifiable criteria.</span><span class='zh'>jevals 是一个展示性项目，用类型化、确定性的判定来取代易出错的 LLM 评审，目标是实现可重复的评估。它将评估从模糊的提示对比，转向更严格、可核验的标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/camilochs/exacttex" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExactTex. A gradually-typed superset of LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>ExactTex：LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExactTex is a gradually-typed superset of LaTeX that adds type annotations to macros and content, enabling stronger tooling and early error detection in LaTeX documents.</span><span class='zh'>ExactTex 是 LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集，为宏和内容添加类型注解，以提升工具链的健壮性与早期错误检测。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExactTex is a gradually-typed superset of LaTeX that adds type annotations to macros and content, enabling stronger tooling and early error detection in LaTeX documents. It aims to improve reliability and developer productivity in complex typeset documents.</span><span class='zh'>ExactTex 是 LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集，为宏和内容添加类型注解，以提升工具链的健壮性与早期错误检测。目标是在复杂排版场景中提高可靠性和开发效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/09/04/top_stories_09042026'><span class='en'>Sep 4</span><span class='zh'>9月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>26</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521631' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>9</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49915484" data-id="49915484" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="25" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49915484" data-time="1790808524">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/edgcpp/compiler" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1915</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/edgcpp/compiler" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EDG C++ Compiler is open source</span><span class='zh'>EDG C++ 编译器开源</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EDG&#x27;s C++ compiler frontend has been open-sourced on GitHub, giving researchers and tool builders access to a mature parsing pipeline that underpins many commercial toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>EDG 的 C++ 编译器前端已在 GitHub 开源，开发者和研究者现在可以访问成熟的解析管线，其广泛支撑着多家商用工具链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EDG&#x27;s C++ compiler frontend has been open-sourced on GitHub, giving researchers and tool builders access to a mature parsing pipeline that underpins many commercial toolchains. The move lowers barriers for experimentation with C++ frontends, static analysis, and language tooling, and could influence compiler ecosystem dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>EDG 的 C++ 编译器前端已在 GitHub 开源，开发者和研究者现在可以访问成熟的解析管线，其广泛支撑着多家商用工具链。此举降低了对 C++ 前端、静态分析和语言工具开发的门槛，可能影响编译器生态的演进与竞争格局。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/09/30/top_stories_09302026'><span class='en'>Sep 30</span><span class='zh'>9月30日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>25</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49915484' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-49802789" data-id="49802789" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Hardware|Code" data-score="23" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49802789" data-time="1790090406">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/General-Instinct/InstinctFlash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: InstinctFlash – High-Performance Serving Runtime for Robotics Models</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：InstinctFlash——用于机器人模型的高性能服务运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>InstinctFlash delivers a high-performance serving runtime tailored for robotics models, enabling low-latency inference and efficient deployment on edge hardware.</span><span class='zh'>InstinctFlash 提供面向机器人模型的高性能服务运行时，实现低延迟推理并高效部署在边缘硬件上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>InstinctFlash delivers a high-performance serving runtime tailored for robotics models, enabling low-latency inference and efficient deployment on edge hardware. The project targets real-time robotics workloads and seamless integration with existing robotics stacks.</span><span class='zh'>InstinctFlash 提供面向机器人模型的高性能服务运行时，实现低延迟推理并高效部署在边缘硬件上。该项目聚焦实时机器人工作负载，并与现有机器人生态无缝集成。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DAPO is an open-source RL system from ByteDance Seed and Tsinghua AIR, aiming to provide scalable RL tooling and benchmarks.</span><span class='zh'>DAPO 是由字节跳动种子实验室与清华 AIR 共同开发的开源强化学习系统，旨在提供可扩展的 RL 工具链与基准。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DAPO is an open-source RL system from ByteDance Seed and Tsinghua AIR, aiming to provide scalable RL tooling and benchmarks. It signals ongoing industry-academia collaboration to democratize access to reinforcement learning research.</span><span class='zh'>DAPO 是由字节跳动种子实验室与清华 AIR 共同开发的开源强化学习系统，旨在提供可扩展的 RL 工具链与基准。它体现了行业与学术界的持续协作，推动让 RL 研究更广泛地开放。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/papers/2605.07019" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LensVLM-9B by Apple</span><span class='zh'>苹果发布 LensVLM-9B</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple releases LensVLM-9B, a 9-billion-parameter vision-language model designed for efficient, on-device inference and privacy-aware multimodal tasks.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 LensVLM-9B，一款 9 亿参数的视觉-语言模型，强调高效推理和隐私友好多模态任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple releases LensVLM-9B, a 9-billion-parameter vision-language model designed for efficient, on-device inference and privacy-aware multimodal tasks. It signals Apple’s push to bring advanced multimodal AI closer to hardware and users, likely influencing future iOS features and developer tooling.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 LensVLM-9B，一款 9 亿参数的视觉-语言模型，强调高效推理和隐私友好多模态任务。此举标志着苹果将先进多模态 AI 推向硬件端的布局，可能影响未来的 iOS 功能和开发者工具。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Obscura now supports native rendering, removing the need for Chromium.</span><span class='zh'>宣布 Obscura 已实现原生渲染，摆脱对 Chromium 的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Obscura now supports native rendering, removing the need for Chromium. This promises privacy gains, lower memory usage, and potential performance improvements for a lightweight, open-source browser.</span><span class='zh'>宣布 Obscura 已实现原生渲染，摆脱对 Chromium 的依赖。这一改动可能带来隐私保护、内存占用与性能方面的提升，推动这款轻量开源浏览器的发展。</span></p>
