<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2015</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0xAX/BootLife" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Conway&#x27;s Game of Life in boot sector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：引导扇区中的康威生命游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact boot-sector implementation of Conway&#x27;s Game of Life demonstrates how far self-contained code can go, running directly on hardware without an OS.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用引导扇区实现的康威生命游戏的极小型代码，能够在没有操作系统的裸机上运行，展示了低级编程的简洁性与极致的空间利用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact boot-sector implementation of Conway&#x27;s Game of Life demonstrates how far self-contained code can go, running directly on hardware without an OS. It&#x27;s a playful reminder of the elegance of low-level programming and space-efficient problem solving.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用引导扇区实现的康威生命游戏的极小型代码，能够在没有操作系统的裸机上运行，展示了低级编程的简洁性与极致的空间利用。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Dillonsmart/docket" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2027</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Dillonsmart/docket" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Docket – Per-commit evidence records for agent-written code</span><span class='zh'>Docket——为代理编写代码提供逐次提交证据记录</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Docket proposes per-commit evidence records to trace decisions made by code produced by autonomous agents, enabling provenance, accountability, and reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>Docket 提议为由自主代理生成的代码提供逐次提交证据记录，以追踪决策来源、提升可追溯性和可重复性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Docket proposes per-commit evidence records to trace decisions made by code produced by autonomous agents, enabling provenance, accountability, and reproducibility. The project aims to make AI-assisted coding auditable by storing verifiable records alongside commits.</span><span class='zh'>Docket 提议为由自主代理生成的代码提供逐次提交证据记录，以追踪决策来源、提升可追溯性和可重复性。该项目通过在提交中附加可验证记录，使 AI 辅助编码具备可审计性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alegrey91/vex8s" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Suppress vulnerabilities applying Kubernetes context to scans</span><span class='zh'>在漏洞扫描中应用 Kubernetes 上下文以抑制误报</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vex8s proposes applying Kubernetes context to scans to suppress vulnerabilities—reducing noise and aligning findings with cluster reality.</span><span class='zh'>Vex8s 提议在漏洞扫描中应用 Kubernetes 上下文以抑制误报，使发现结果与集群实际环境保持一致。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vex8s proposes applying Kubernetes context to scans to suppress vulnerabilities—reducing noise and aligning findings with cluster reality. The technique helps security teams focus on meaningful risk while preserving coverage.</span><span class='zh'>Vex8s 提议在漏洞扫描中应用 Kubernetes 上下文以抑制误报，使发现结果与集群实际环境保持一致。该方法有助于安全团队将精力集中在实质风险上，同时保持覆盖面。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Fenish/decky" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2049</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Fenish/decky" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decky: Open-source DIY stream deck</span><span class='zh'>Decky：开源DIY流媒体控制板</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Decky, an open-source, DIY alternative to commercial stream decks with a plugin ecosystem and cross-platform support.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Decky，这是一个开源的 DIY 流媒体控制板替代方案，具备插件生态和跨平台支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Decky, an open-source, DIY alternative to commercial stream decks with a plugin ecosystem and cross-platform support. It enables custom macro keys for streaming, productivity, and gaming, driven by community contributions.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Decky，这是一个开源的 DIY 流媒体控制板替代方案，具备插件生态和跨平台支持。它为流媒体、生产力和游戏等场景提供自定义快捷键，并由社区贡献推动发展。</span></p>
